\documentclass[10pt]{article}

\setlength{\jot}{4pt}
\begin{document}
\begin{center}\Large SRI: N10LO\end{center}
The spectra are evaluated at $-k_*\eta_*=1$, through relative amplitude
order $p=10$. The Hubble-flow parameters are denoted by $\epsilon_j$
and $M_{\rm Pl}$ is the reduced Planck mass. Coefficients are generated
numerically from the normalized mode equations, using the iterative algebra.
The convention for the spectral derivatives is
\[
\alpha_n^A=\frac{d^{n+1}\ln P_A}{d(\ln k)^{n+1}},\qquad
\alpha_0^s=n_s-1,\qquad\alpha_0^t=n_t,\qquad r=P_t/P_s.
\]
\section{Spectra}

\subsection{Scalar spectrum}
\[P_s(k_*)=\frac{H_*^2}{8\pi^2M_{\rm Pl}^2\epsilon_{1*}}\left[\sum_{d=0}^{10}F_{s,d}+O(\mathrm{SR}^{11})\right].\]
\paragraph{Relative order 0.}
\begin{align*}
F_{s,0} &= 1.0
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Relative order 1.}
\begin{align*}
F_{s,1} &= 0.72963715453852 \epsilon_{2} - 0.54072569092296 \epsilon_{1}
\end{align*}

F_{s,2} &= 1.5402686460338 \epsilon_{1}^{2} + 0.49988573877771 \epsilon_{2}^{2} \\

\section{Consistency relations}
\subsection{$n=0$}
For $p=10$, the absolute cutoff is $W=11$:
\[\alpha_{0}^t=\sum_{d=1}^{11}R_{0,d}+O(\mathrm{SR}^{12}).\]
\paragraph{Absolute order 1.}
\begin{align*}
R_{0,1} &=  - 0.125 r
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 2.}
\begin{align*}
R_{0,2} &= 0.0078125 r^{2} + 0.125 \alpha_{0}^s r
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 3.}
\begin{align*}
R_{0,3} &=  - 0.0030712246746565 r^{3} + 0.125 \alpha_{1}^s r \\
&{}  - 0.10280837917801 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r - 0.041327094794504 \alpha_{0}^s r^{2}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 4.}
\begin{align*}
R_{0,4} &= 0.0014926374043055 r^{4} + 0.125 \alpha_{2}^s r \\
&{}  + 0.065244100954277 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{3} r + 0.024554893470954 \alpha_{0}^s r^{3} \\
&{}  + 0.10320649151137 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r^{2} - 0.060109233906372 \alpha_{1}^s r^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.23087387125098 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{1}^s r
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 5.}
\begin{align*}
R_{0,5} &=  - 0.00093280100983415 r^{5} + 0.125 \alpha_{3}^s r \\
&{}  + 0.04315951309091 \alpha_{1}^s r^{3} - 0.038536574146577 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{4} r \\
&{}  - 0.018206214478227 \alpha_{0}^s r^{4} - 0.077020534433109 \alpha_{2}^s r^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.14103704832205 \left(\alpha_{1}^s\right)^{2} r - 0.18695785481671 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{3} r^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.10777687087114 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r^{3} + 0.33322659757276 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{1}^s r^{2} \\
&{}  + 0.25691700713838 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} \alpha_{1}^s r - 0.24116427546487 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{2}^s r
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 6.}
\begin{align*}
R_{0,6} &= 0.00070298503785145 r^{6} + 0.125 \alpha_{4}^s r \\
&{}  + 0.020902477635746 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{5} r + 0.015994052232973 \alpha_{0}^s r^{5} \\
&{}  + 0.065362321729807 \alpha_{2}^s r^{3} + 0.28417656846613 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{4} r^{2} \\
&{}  + 0.30605471800346 \left(\alpha_{1}^s\right)^{2} r^{2} + 0.34969699644176 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{3} r^{3} \\
&{}  + 0.12218812205202 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r^{4} - 0.093222530607224 \alpha_{3}^s r^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.037217907105313 \alpha_{1}^s r^{4} + 0.4688109212146 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{2}^s r^{2} \\
&{}  + 0.37293859690816 \alpha_{0}^s \left(\alpha_{1}^s\right)^{2} r + 0.29046664127673 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} \alpha_{2}^s r \\
&{}  - 0.22801627965519 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{3} \alpha_{1}^s r - 1.015918735308 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} \alpha_{1}^s r^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.31399132156735 \alpha_{1}^s \alpha_{2}^s r - 0.42145582743858 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{1}^s r^{3} \\
&{}  - 0.24578024485779 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{3}^s r
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 7.}
\begin{align*}
R_{0,7} &=  - 0.00061515419372413 r^{7} + 0.125 \alpha_{5}^s r \\
&{}  + 0.03707739098979 \alpha_{1}^s r^{5} + 0.19980707266399 \left(\alpha_{1}^s\right)^{3} r \\
&{}  + 0.091144689305009 \alpha_{3}^s r^{3} - 0.016058051152622 \alpha_{0}^s r^{6} \\
&{}  - 0.011049369381434 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{6} r - 0.18310571021651 \left(\alpha_{2}^s\right)^{2} r \\
&{}  - 0.94736847967917 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{4} r^{3} - 0.064533532306596 \alpha_{2}^s r^{4} \\
&{}  - 0.60450331655168 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{3} r^{4} - 0.15064645355686 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r^{5} \\
&{}  - 0.38746735293638 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{5} r^{2} - 0.10911851595073 \alpha_{4}^s r^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.46539756860253 \left(\alpha_{1}^s\right)^{2} r^{3} + 0.55277396843725 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{1}^s r^{4} \\
&{}  + 2.2882998446743 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} \alpha_{1}^s r^{3} + 0.61514830647938 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{3}^s r^{2} \\
&{}  + 0.17617169912302 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{4} \alpha_{1}^s r + 0.97696202212274 \alpha_{1}^s \alpha_{2}^s r^{2} \\
&{}  + 2.3093282152193 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{3} \alpha_{1}^s r^{2} + 0.30878621388554 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} \alpha_{3}^s r \\
&{}  - 1.5812089463636 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} \alpha_{2}^s r^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.56258489487217 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} \left(\alpha_{1}^s\right)^{2} r - 0.33131930046622 \alpha_{1}^s \alpha_{3}^s r \\
&{}  - 0.24794812760585 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{4}^s r - 2.0996792408229 \alpha_{0}^s \left(\alpha_{1}^s\right)^{2} r^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.28144573547165 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{3} \alpha_{2}^s r - 0.7030684429864 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{2}^s r^{3} \\
&{}  + 0.90387358971276 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{1}^s \alpha_{2}^s r
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 8.}
